# Журнал разработки

> Новые записи добавляются ТОЛЬКО последней строкой таблицы в самом конце файла. Существующие строки не редактируются и не удаляются. В начале файла лежит исторический блок с обратным порядком дат — его не трогать. Полное правило: раздел «Правило ведения журналов» в `docs/OZON_PLAN.md`.

Зафиксировано, что интерфейс сделан без дополнительных кнопок: достройка запускается автоматически после подтверждения заявки. В этап F внесены три дефекта, вскрытых живым регрессом: декомпозированная кириллица в именах файлов Google Диска, удержание резерва заявкой, удалённой до первого опроса, и незакрытые журнальные строки заявок, отменённых после начала отслеживания. | Живой регресс удаления отменённых записей возможен не раньше 28.08.2026; многокластерная заявка через новый мастер вживую не прогонялась |

| 2026-08-20 | Item 26: composite Ozon read, and three cache bugs | `Code.gs`, `server.ts`, `src/store/useWarehouseStore.ts`, `src/components/Dashboard.tsx` | The premise of item 26 turned out to be wrong: start-up is one request, not seventeen. getOzonSales alone takes 9-13 s because the whole 3194-row sales sheet is read every time and 57% of it is archive rows the date window discards. Bundling five reads into one changed nothing measurable (9.8 s vs 10.6 s, inside the noise); what did change everything was repairing the cache. The proxy tracks actions in THREE separate lists — getCacheTtlMs, CACHE_INVALIDATION and READ_ONLY_ACTIONS — and I registered the new composite in none of them at first, which is two of the three bugs below. | BUG 1, mine: an unknown action gets TTL 0, so the composite was uncached and start-up went from 12.7 to 14.5 s. BUG 2, mine: an action missing from READ_ONLY_ACTIONS counts as a write, so every cache miss on the composite wiped the entire cache. BUG 3, long-standing: getLastPurchasePrices was never in READ_ONLY_ACTIONS even though the dashboard fires it on every load — the whole cache was being wiped on every single start-up, which is why caching had never helped. A comment now states the three jobs that list does, so the next person adding an action does not repeat this. |
| 2026-08-20 | Item 26: sales sheet split into a weekly sheet and an archive sheet | `Code.gs`, `tests/apps-script/*` | getOzonSales reads its sheet whole on every start-up, and 1805 of 3194 rows were 28-day archive blocks that the date window always discards. The compacted blocks now live in «Продажи Ozon Архив». KEY FINDING that shaped the design: the archive is NOT frozen history — saveOzonSales rebuilds it on every sync by compacting weekly rows that age out of the 13-week weekly zone, and it re-aggregates existing blocks each time. So the write path reads BOTH sheets and splits the result again at write time, while the start-up path reads only the weekly sheet. | No manual data migration: on the first sync after deployment the legacy archive rows are still in the main sheet, get read like any other row and are written back to the archive sheet. Until that first sync the main sheet still holds both zones, which is harmless — the date window discards them anyway. NOTICED IN PASSING, worth remembering: the weekly zone is 13 weeks and the trend window is also 13 weeks, so the trend has exactly ZERO margin — raising trendWeeks above 13 would silently give it fewer full weeks, because week 14 is already compacted and dropped by the days !== 7 filter. |
| 2026-08-20 | Item 26 stages A2 and C: one copy of the reference data, and an unchained boot | `src/store/useWarehouseStore.ts`, `src/App.tsx`, `src/components/Dashboard.tsx`, `src/components/OzonStocksTab.tsx`, `src/components/OzonSettingsModal.tsx` | A2: Ozon settings and the cluster reference book lived in the local state of two screens, each fetching its own copy; they moved to the store and are filled by the composite call. C: nothing was sent until verifySession returned, roughly three seconds of dead time, although the token is already in the browser and Apps Script verifies the session on every read anyway. Reads now start alongside the check, and since the role is persisted next to the token an admin also starts the composite Ozon read at once. | Two consequences worth remembering. FIRST: because the tab no longer refetches settings on mount, saving them has to re-read explicitly — otherwise the screen would keep calculating on the old values until a full page reload. SECOND, the trap: the boot flag was first read through a store subscription and the effect closed over the value from the render BEFORE checkSession ran, so it sent a duplicate getInitialData, and keeping the flag in the dependency list re-ran the effect and fetched the archive twice. Imperative getState() reads are the right tool for a "has this already started" flag. |

| 2026-08-20 | Rule 2.2a: bulk exploration goes to a Sonnet agent | `ТЕХНИЧЕСКИЙ_БРИФ_Складской_учет.md` | Owner's decision after a measurement, not a guess. Session `28af96ec`: 1 570 requests, 447.8 M input tokens, average context 285 000 tokens per request, and 60.3% of the material in the conversation was `Bash` output — file reads and searches. So a wide search now goes to `Explore` launched with an explicit `model: sonnet`: the intermediate output stays in the agent, only the conclusion returns. The rule carries its own threshold, because a launch costs 15–30 k tokens of the agent's own context: one grep, a known path or a few lines are still done inline. It also states that the agent must be asked for the ANSWER — paths, line numbers, a short conclusion — since a delegated read that returns a full listing saves nothing. `Explore` added to the roles table in 2.1, and the «reading and diagnosis stay with the orchestrator» line qualified with «only while they are small». REJECTED IN THE SAME MEASUREMENT: a dedicated agent for screenshots, which the owner asked about — 7 screenshots in the whole session, 1 845 tokens, 0.49% of input even counting the re-read on every later request; one agent launch costs ten times that. | The rule is advisory, like every rule in the brief; nothing enforces it. |
| 2026-08-20 | Item 45: hand-added clusters and articles in the supply window | `src/lib/ozonSupplyLines.ts`, `src/lib/ozonSupplyLines.test.ts`, `src/components/OzonSupplyModal.tsx`, `src/components/OzonStocksTab.tsx`, `docs/OZON_PLAN.md` | Exploration was delegated to an `Explore` agent on sonnet under the new rule 2.2a, and its findings were checked against the files before any code was written. THE KEY FINDING that shaped the design: the draft payload, the availability check and the journal's itemsJSON all read ONE array, `activeRows`, which was a pure filter of the `rows` prop. So the whole feature fits in one seam — `addedRows` state, `allRows = rows + addedRows`, and `activeRows` filtering `allRows`. Nothing downstream needed a special case, and the reserve on «Мой склад» works for hand-added lines with no extra code at all, because `buildPendingSupplies` reads the generic `{article, clusterId, qty}` out of `itemsJSON`. `removeCluster` was pointed at `allRows` so it can also drop a hand-added cluster. THE CEILING is a new pure function `capForSupplyLine`: free stock for the article MINUS what the other lines of the same supply already take, since one article can sit in several clusters. Checked against the Ozon API index of 466 methods: `DraftCreateInfo` (/v2/draft/create/info) — the very method our proxy polls in `server.ts` after `/v1/draft/multi-cluster/create` — returns clusters, warehouses, availability, `total_rank` and `total_score`, and not one monetary field. A full-text sweep for «стоимость / тариф / логистика / цена» inside the supply section returns zero methods. `total_score`/`total_rank` are a warehouse rating and rank, easy to mistake for a price and dimensionless. The only place real logistics money appears is after the fact, in `/v3/finance/transaction/list`. Not to be confused with `/v5/product/info/prices`, which carries logistics tariffs for SELLING — delivery to the customer — not for delivering a supply to a cluster.
